### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは速さを優先してデータを届けるだけの通信です。

TCPは届いたかを確認して再送し、順番も揃えますが、UDPはそれをせず、遅延や欠落を気にしない代わりに軽く速く動きます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales y actualizar tu rama con los cambios de la base antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida, porque reescribir historia publicada da problemas a otras personas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠、有序、面向连接的传输，会确认、重传并保证数据顺序；UDP 是不保证送达与顺序的无连接传输，开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch before opening a PR, to keep history linear and tidy. Merge when the branch is already shared or others have built on it, since rebasing rewrites commits they may depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接给文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

Bun 会把参数当作路径过滤，所以写文件名的一部分也能匹配，比如 `bun test foo` 会跑所有路径里带 `foo` 的测试文件。

要只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例名"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before it is applied to the main data files. If the system crashes mid-write, it replays that log on restart so committed changes survive and half-finished ones can be rolled back.
```

  [an app card under the message: made "周五京沪高铁票价", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1880 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">还没查</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">每天早上更新</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">班次</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c];
    });
  }

  function render(d) {
    var t = (d && d.trains) || [];
    var sorted = t.slice().sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });
    var under = sorted.filter(function (x) { return x.price < 600; });

    if (!t.length) {
      cnt.textContent = '还没查';
      cnt.className = 'k-sum-value k-none';
      sub.textContent = '每天早上更新';
      list.innerHTML = '<div class="k-empty">还没有班次，每天早上会更新。</div>';
      return;
    }

    cnt.className = 'k-sum-value';
    cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = under.length ? '600 元以下的班次' : '今天没有 600 元以下的班次';

    list.innerHTML = sorted.map(function (x) {
      var left = x.left != null ? '余 ' + x.left + ' 张' : '';
      var seat = x.seat ? x.seat : '';
      var sub2 = [seat, left].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
      var cls = x.price < 600 ? '' : ' style="color:var(--d-muted)"';
      return '<div class="k-row"' + cls + '>' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(x.no) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(sub2) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(x.price) + '</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');
  }

  render(ducter.get('trains'));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get('trains')); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","seat":"二等座","price":553,"left":12},{"no":"G3","seat":"二等座","price":553,"left":4},{"no":"G7","seat":"二等座","price":626}]}

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with the nightly runs so far. Night 3 is 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, and it fills in each night from the task.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1599 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">Writes per second, p99 latency</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="runs"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){ return n == null ? '—' : Math.round(n / 1000) + 'k'; }
function render(){
  var runs = ducter.get('runs') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('runs');
  list.innerHTML = '';
  if (!runs.length) {
    var e = document.createElement('div');
    e.className = 'k-empty';
    e.textContent = 'Each night’s result will list here.';
    list.appendChild(e);
    return;
  }
  var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
  document.getElementById('latest').textContent = fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s';
  document.getElementById('latest-sub').textContent =
    last.night + ' · p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms' + (last.stalls == null ? '' : ' · ' + last.stalls + ' compaction stalls');
  runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function(r){
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' ms · ' + (r.stalls == null ? 'stalls —' : r.stalls + ' compaction stalls') + '</div>' +
      '<div class="k-row-value">' + fmt(r.writes) + '/s</div>';
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  ducter.summary(last.night + ': ' + fmt(last.writes) + ' writes/s, p99 ' + last.p99 + ' ms');
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":"Night 3","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

.k-del{display:none;margin-top:8px;background:none;border:1px solid var(--d-bad);color:var(--d-bad);border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:13px}
.k-row.open .k-del{display:inline-block}

.k-input,.k-num{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:9px;border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:8px;background:var(--d-sunken);color:var(--d-ink);font-size:16px}
.k-btn.primary{width:100%;margin-top:6px;padding:10px;border:0;border-radius:10px;background:var(--d-accent);color:var(--d … t) + '</span></div><button class="k-del" data-del="' + x.id + '">删除</button></div>').join('') +
      '</section>';
  }).join('');
  ducter.summary(items.length + ' 笔,合计 ' + money(total));
}

async function load() {
  const v = await ducter.get(K);
  items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
  render();
}

async function save() {
  await ducter.set(K, items);
  render();
}

document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const day = document.getElementById('fday').value.trim();
  const item = document.getElementById('fitem').value.trim();
  const amt = parseFloat(document.getElementById('famt').value);
  if (!day || !(amt > 0)) return;
  items.push({id: Date.now(), day, item, amt});
  document.getElementById('fitem').value = '';
  document.getElementById('famt').value = '';
  await save();
});

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if (del) {
    const id = Number(del.dataset.del);
    items = items.filter(x => x.id !== id);
    await save();
    return;
  }
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (row) row.classList.toggle('open');
});

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

.k-sub{color:var(--d-muted);font-size:13px;margin-bottom:10px}
.k-strip{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr);gap:6px}
.k-chip{border:1px solid var(--d-line);background:var(--d-sunken);color:var(--d-muted);border-radius:8px;padding:7px 0 6px;font-size:13px;cursor:pointer;text-align:center;font-family:inherit}
.k-chip .dow{display:block;font-size:10px;opacity:.75}
.k-chip[aria- … BITS){
    const list=ticks[id]||[];
    const st=stats(list);
    if(st.best>bestAll)bestAll=st.best;
    if(list.includes(todayK))doneToday++;
    html+='<div class="k-card"><div class="k-h">'+name+'</div><div class="k-sub">Streak '+st.cur+' · best '+st.best+'</div><div class="k-strip">';
    for(const d of days){
      const k=keyOf(d);
      const on=list.includes(k);
      html+='<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="'+on+'" data-h="'+id+'" data-d="'+k+'"><span class="dow">'+d.toLocaleDateString('en-US',{weekday:'narrow'})+'</span>'+d.getDate()+'</button>';
    }
    html+='</div></div>';
  }
  document.getElementById('list').innerHTML=html;
  document.getElementById('today').textContent=doneToday+' of '+HABITS.length;
  document.getElementById('sumsub').textContent=doneToday===HABITS.length?'All three done today':'ticked today · best streak '+bestAll;
  ducter.summary(doneToday+' of '+HABITS.length+' ticked today');
}
document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const b=e.target.closest('button[data-h]');
  if(b)toggle(b.dataset.h,b.dataset.d);
});
async function load(){ticks=(await ducter.get('ticks'))||{};render()}
load();
ducter.onChange(load);
</script>
; its data now (what it opens with): {}

      row.append(del);
    }

function drawCats() {
  const box = $('cats');
  box.textContent = '';

async function save() { await ducter.set('expenses', items); render(); }

async function remove(id) {
  items = items.filter(e => e.id !== id);
  openId = null;
  await save();
}

$('add').onclick = async () => {
  const amount = parseFloat(($('amt').value || '').replace(',', '.'));
  const what = ($('what').value || '').trim();
  if (!(amount > 0) || !what) return;
  items.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6), amount, what, cat, date: new Date().toISOString() });
  $('amt').value = '';
  $('what').value = '';
  await save();
};

async function load() {
  items = (await ducter.get('expenses')) || [];
  render();
}

ducter.onChange(load);
drawCats();
load();

### app-baby-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 做个小应用记宝宝喝奶。今天早上 6:10 喝了 120ml，9:05 喝了 90ml
  [partner message] 做好了，今天的两次已经记上：6:10 120ml、9:05 90ml，合计 210ml。以后记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "宝宝喝奶", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2469 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">今天合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">0 ml</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">今天的奶</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label for="t">时间</label><input class="k-input" id="t" type="text" placeholder="HH:MM"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label for="ml">奶量 (ml)</label><input class="k-num" id="ml" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="例如 120"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  function pad(n){return (n<10?'0':'')+n;}
  function today(){var d=new Date();return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate());}
  function nowHM(){var d=new Date();return pad(d.getHours())+':'+pad(d.getMinutes());}
  var all=[];
  function render(){
    var t=today();
    var feeds=all.filter(function(f){return f.date===t;}).sort(function(a,b){return a.t<b.t?-1:1;});
    var sum=feeds.reduce(function(s,f){return s+Number(f.ml);},0);
    document.getElementById('total').textContent=sum+' ml';
    document.getElementById('sub').textContent=feeds.length?(feeds.length+' 次 · 最近 '+feeds[feeds.length-1].t):'还没记';
    var list=document.getElementById('list');
    list.innerHTML='';
    if(!feeds.length){
      var e=document.createElement('div');e.className='k-empty';e.textContent='今天还没有记录';list.appendChild(e);
    }
    feeds.forEach(function(f){
      var r=document.createElement('div');r.className='k-row';
      r.innerHTML='<div class="k-row-main">'+f.t+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+f.ml+' ml</div>';
      list.appendChild(r);
    });
    ducter.summary(feeds.length?(feeds.length+' 次,合计 '+sum+'ml'):'今天还没记');
  }
  ducter.get('feeds').then(function(v){all=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];render();});
  ducter.onChange(function(){ducter.get('feeds').then(function(v){all=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];render();});});
  document.getElementById('t').value=nowHM();
  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click',function(){
    var t=document.getElementById('t').value.trim();
    var ml=parseFloat(document.getElementById('ml').value);
    if(!/^\d{1,2}:\d{2}$/.test(t)||!(ml>0))return;
    all.push({date:today(),t:t,ml:ml});
    ducter.set('feeds',all);
    document.getElementById('ml').value='';
    document.getElementById('t').value=nowHM();
    render();
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"date":"2026-11-23","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"date":"2026-11-23","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs, and you tick each one off as it lands. It's on your shelf.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2127 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Waiting on someone</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="left">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div>
</div>

<h2 class="k-h">Pull requests</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>
</main>
<script>
(function(){
  var prs = [];
  var landed = {};
  function esc(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function(c){ return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;"}[c]; }); }
  function render(){
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    list.innerHTML = "";
    var n = 0;
    prs.forEach(function(p){
      var on = !!landed[p.id];
      if(on) n++;
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      row.style.opacity = on ? "0.5" : "1";
      row.innerHTML = '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + on + '" aria-label="Landed">' + (on ? "&#10003;" : "&#9675;") + '</button>' +
        '<div class="k-row-main"><div>' + esc(p.title) + '</div><div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(p.status) + '</div></div>';
      row.querySelector("button").addEventListener("click", function(){
        landed[p.id] = !landed[p.id];
        if(window.ducter && ducter.set){ ducter.set("landed", landed); }
        if(window.ducter && ducter.summary){ ducter.summary(Object.keys(landed).filter(function(k){return landed[k];}).length + " of " + prs.length + " landed"); }
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var open = prs.length - n;
    document.getElementById("left").textContent = open + " open";
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = n + " landed · tick each one off as it lands";
  }
  function load(){
    var p = window.ducter && ducter.get ? ducter.get("prs") : null;
    var l = window.ducter && ducter.get ? ducter.get("landed") : null;
    Promise.all([p, l]).then(function(v){
      prs = Array.isArray(v[0]) ? v[0] : [];
      landed = (v[1] && typeof v[1] === "object") ? v[1] : {};
      render();
    });
  }
  load();
  if(window.ducter && ducter.onChange){ ducter.onChange(load); }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","title":"wey-gu/ducter#412 · compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","title":"nowledge-co/mem#10287 · retry on 502","status":"On CI, failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","title":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 · flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","title":"wey-gu/ducter#418 · apps bar","status":"Draft"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>

<script>
(function(){
var cat="交通";
function $(id){return document.getElementById(id)}
function esc(s){return String(s==null?"":s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;","\"":"&quot;"}[c]})}
function draw(){
  var xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  var total=xs.reduce(function(s,x){return s+(+x.amt||0)},0);
  var t=$("total");
  i … -sum-value k-none";t.textContent="还没记"}
  $("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"记了花销就会显示在这里";
  var list=$("list");
  if(!xs.length){list.innerHTML='<p class="k-empty">还没有花销，展开下面「记一笔」添加。</p>';}
  else{list.innerHTML=xs.map(function(x){
    return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.cat)+' · '+esc(x.trip)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+(x.note?' · '+esc(x.note):'')+'</div><div class="k-row-value">¥'+(+x.amt||0).toLocaleString("zh-CN")+'</div></div>'
  }).join("")}
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+total.toLocaleString("zh-CN"):"还没记");
}
Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip"),function(b){
  b.onclick=function(){
    cat=b.getAttribute("data-c");
    Array.prototype.forEach.call(document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip"),function(o){o.setAttribute("aria-pressed",o===b?"true":"false")});
  };
});
$("save").onclick=function(){
  var amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt){return}
  var xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value||"北京出差",day:$("day").value,cat:cat,amt:amt,note:""});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
